# Commands · L · part 6

1,468 entries, ll-rag to login. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ll-rag**: **One command, complete guidance.** Luna RAG provides intelligent semantic code search with a single conversational interface. No complex commands to remember - just ask questions and let Luna guide you. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-rag.md))
- **ll-rag-guided**: **One command, one guided experience. No need to remember multiple commands.** ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-rag-guided.md))
- **ll-rag-upgrade**: **Usage:** ```bash /luna-rag upgrade ``` ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-rag-upgrade.md))
- **ll-rails-doctor**: Run `npx rails-doctor@latest` (real package) plus brakeman (security), bundler-audit (CVEs), rubocop (lint), and a Luna heuristic layer for n+1, fat-controller, missing strong params, and CSRF gaps. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-rails-doctor.md))
- **ll-react-doctor**: Run `npx react-doctor@latest` against the current repo to diagnose security, performance, correctness, accessibility, bundle-size, and architecture issues, then route each finding through `/ll-no-bluf` and open a fix-PR. Wraps millionco/react-doctor. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-react-doctor.md))
- **ll-readme-sync**: Regenerate README.md (and optionally the website skills/lexicon pages) from the actual contents of commands/, agents/, and skills/. Keeps counters honest, builds the command index, and prevents docs from drifting out of sync with code. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-readme-sync.md))
- **ll-record**: Auto-generate demo videos of your features — screen recording, AI narration, background music, editing, and publishing ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-record.md))
- **ll-refactor**: Smart refactoring — split files, extract shared logic, rename across codebase, enforce size limits ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-refactor.md))
- **ll-requirements**: Analyze the project codebase and generate comprehensive requirements document ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-requirements.md))
- **ll-review**: Perform comprehensive code review of implemented features ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-review.md))
- **ll-rollback**: Generate rollback procedures, down migrations, feature flags, canary config ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-rollback.md))
- **ll-routemap**: Build a complete routing and navigation map with pages, params, guards, and auth matrix ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-routemap.md))
- **ll-rules**: Apply strict coding session rules — 100-line file cap, full test coverage, Playwright e2e for every feature ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-rules.md))
- **ll-rust-doctor**: Run `npx rust-doctor@latest` (real package) plus cargo clippy, cargo audit, cargo deny, cargo udeps. - **ll-sec-dast**: Dynamic application security testing — runs Nuclei + ZAP baseline against a live URL to find runtime vulns ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-dast.md))

- **ll-sec-deps**: SCA via OSV-Scanner (Google) + license-checker. Catches vulnerable + non-compliant deps across npm/pypi/maven/cargo/go. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-deps.md))
- **ll-sec-fuzz**: Coverage-guided fuzzing per language — Jazzer.js (JS/TS), Atheris (Python), go-fuzz / native libFuzzer (Go) ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-fuzz.md))

- **ll-sec-lifecycle**: One-shot installer that wires every Luna security command into git hooks, GitHub Actions, and cron. Idempotent. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-lifecycle.md))
- **ll-sec-pr**: PR/push CI bundle (~30s). Runs SAST + secret-history + dep audit + license check. Posts findings to PR comment. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-pr.md))
- **ll-sec-precommit**: Fast (&lt;5s) staged-changes scan run by git pre-commit hook. Catches secrets + obvious lint-level security issues before commit. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-precommit.md))
- **ll-sec-push**: Run the entire security lifecycle in one shot. Pre-push umbrella. Fast / Full / CI modes. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-push.md))
- **ll-sec-report**: Aggregate all per-tool SARIF/JSON outputs into one human + machine readable summary. Trend over time. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-report.md))

- **ll-sec-sast**: Static analysis with Semgrep using OWASP Top 10 + CWE Top 25 + language-specific rulesets ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-sast.md))

- **ll-sec-secrets**: Detect leaked secrets in source, history, and staged changes using gitleaks + trufflehog (verified) ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-secrets.md))
- **ll-sec-sign**: Keyless signing of binaries, images, and SBOMs via Cosign + Sigstore. Produces verifiable attestations. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-sign.md))
- **ll-sec-threat-model**: STRIDE-based threat model from a YAML system description via Threagile. Generates risk register + data-flow diagrams + mitigation tracking. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-sec-threat-model.md))

Composes SpotBugs, OWASP Dependency-Check, Spring Actuator, error-prone, PMD with a Luna Spring-aware heuristic layer. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-spring-doctor.md))

- **ll-svelte-doctor**: Run `npx svelte-doctor-cli@latest` (or `svelte-doctor` if preferred) against the current repo to diagnose state, effects, performance, security, accessibility, architecture, and dead-code issues, then route each finding through `/ll-no-bluf` and open a fix-PR. Wraps the existing Svelte doctor packages — does NOT reinvent. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-svelte-doctor.md))
- **ll-swarm**: Spawn a coordinated multi-agent swarm — parallel coding, reviewing, testing, and deploying with consensus ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm.md))
- **ll-swarm-blackboard**: Scaffold a Blackboard-pattern swarm — every agent reads and writes a typed shared case file (Postgres-backed) instead of passing chat history. Implements pattern §4.5 from blog/swarm-of-agents.md. Use when token cost of message-passing dominates and the case state is structured. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm-blackboard.md))
- **ll-swarm-debate**: Scaffold a Debate/Review/Verifier-pattern swarm — one agent proposes, a critic challenges, a verifier validates evidence and safety before the final answer. Implements pattern §4.4 from blog/swarm-of-agents.md. Recommended for fintech, security, billing, and any production-action context. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm-debate.md))
- **ll-swarm-supervisor**: Scaffold a Supervisor/Manager-pattern swarm — supervisor agent picks the next specialist, specialists return artifacts, verifier gates the final output, executor runs deterministic approved actions only. The safest production pattern from blog/swarm-of-agents.md §4.1. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm-supervisor.md))
- **ll-swarm-vote**: Scaffold a democratic agent swarm with evidence-weighted voting. Three rounds (independent answers, peer review, vote-with-evidence), aggregation by agent weight × confidence × evidence quality (penalised by contradictions + unsupported claims), and verifier veto authority. Implements blog/swarm-vote.md end-to-end. Majority alone does not decide truth. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-swarm-vote.md))
- **ll-terraform-doctor**: Diagnose Terraform / OpenTofu via tflint, tfsec, checkov, terrascan, plus Luna heuristic layer for state drift, over-permissive IAM, and missing tags. ([lunaos-ai/luna-agents](https://github.com/lunaos-ai/luna-agents/blob/HEAD/commands/ll-terraform-doctor.md))
